Skip for high-risk runtime/orchestration work (use systems-engineer), CLI command output (use cli-ux-engineer), prompts and race files (use race-author), tests (use test-engineer), reviews (use code-reviewer), marketing copy (use doc-writer), or catalog/site work (use catalog-builder). Never spends API budget beyond an authorized smoke cap, never touches the Boox device or shared state. Default model sonnet; the Supervisor overrides to opus for hard stories (translate engine, PDF reflow, Readium rendering, sync logic). ([nikogamulin/berilo](https://github.com/nikogamulin/berilo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/task-implementer.md) · ★3)
